Summarizing the results above,

\hbox{var}\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{2}(z,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}=O\{1/(nh^{3})\}
.
Hence
the estimator
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{2}(z,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
satisfies

\displaystyle\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{2}(z,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})={\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}_{2}(z,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})+O_{p}\{(nh^{3})^{-1/2}+h^{2}\}

under conditions

C1
-
C5
.

We give detailed proof for uniform result of (
20
) only. Because
the domain of
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}
is compact, we divide it into rectangular
regions. In each region, the distance between a point
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}
in
this region and the nearest grid point is less than
n^{-2}
. We need only
N\leq Cn^{2}
grid points, where
C
is a constant. Let the grid points be

\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{1},\dots,\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{N}
. Let

\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})=\widehat{E}\{{\bf X}Y(Z)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}
and

\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})=E\{{\bf X}Y(Z)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}
. Then for any

(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
, there exists a
\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}
,
1\leq i\leq N
, such that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|+|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|+|\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|+D_{1}n^{-2},

for an absolute constant
D_{1}
under
Conditions
C1
and
C5
. Thus, for
any
D\geq D_{1}
,

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}(\sup_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})|>2D[h^{2}+\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}(\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|>2D[h^{2}+\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}]-D_{1}n^{-2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}(\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|>D[h^{2}+\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}])

under Condition

C2
. Using
Bernstein’s inequality on
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})
, under Conditions

C1
-
C5
, we have

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}[|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|\geq A\{\hbox{log}n/(nh)\}^{1/2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{\frac{-nA^{2}\hbox{log}n/(nh)}{2D_{2}h^{-1}+2/3AD_{3}(\hbox{log}n)^{1/2}(nh)^{-1/2}}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\exp\left\{\frac{-A^{2}\hbox{log}n}{2D_{2}+2/3AD_{3}(\hbox{log}nh/n)^{1/2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\exp\left(\frac{-A^{2}\hbox{log}n}{2D_{2}+AD_{3}}\right)

for all
A>D_{3}+\sqrt{D_{3}^{2}+4D_{2}}
, where
D_{2}
and
D_{3}
are constants satisfying

\displaystyle\hbox{var}\{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D_{2}}{nh},

\displaystyle\left|\frac{K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}){\bf X}_{i}I(Z_{i}\geq Z)}{1/n\sum_{j=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle D_{3}\text{ with probability 1}.

This leads to

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}[\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-E\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|\geq A\{\hbox{log}n/(nh)\}^{1/2}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2Cn^{2}\exp\left(\frac{-A^{2}\hbox{log}n}{2D_{2}+AD_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2C\exp\left[\{2-A^{2}/(2D_{2}+AD_{3})\}\hbox{log}n\right]\to 0

because
A>D_{3}+\sqrt{D_{3}^{2}+4D_{2}}
.
Combining the above results, for
A_{1}=\max(A,D)
,

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}(\sup_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},\boldsymbol{\beta})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})|>2A_{1}[h^{2}+\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}(\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|>A_{1}[h^{2}+\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\{\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|>A_{1}h^{2}\}+\hbox{pr}([\sup_{\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i}}|\widehat{\boldsymbol{\rho}}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})-\boldsymbol{\rho}(\boldsymbol{\kappa}_{i})|\geq A_{1}\{\hbox{log}n(nh)^{-1}\}^{1/2}])

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle 0.

The uniform convergence results concerning (
21
)-(
24
)
are slightly different because these functions contain the additional component

Z
. Nevertheless, under Condition
C5
, the support of

(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i})
or
(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{j})
is also bounded so we
can similarly divide the region using
N\leq Cn^{2+2}
grid points while the
distance of a point to the nearest grid point is less than
n^{-2}
. The rest of
the analysis can then be similarly carried out as above, then the uniform convergence is established. ∎

3.1.2
Lemma
2

Lemma
2
.

The estimator
\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
has the
expansion

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\times dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}+o_{p}(1),

and
satisfies

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}\to N\{0,\sigma^{2}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}

in distribution when
n\to\infty
for all
t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}

under Conditions

C1
-
C5
, where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})=\int K^{2}(u)du\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}ds.

Proof:

We only need to prove

\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}\to N\{0,\sigma^{2}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}
because

\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\widehat{\Lambda}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}=O_{p}(\sqrt{h})=o_{p}(1)

due to Theorems
1
and

2
.
For notational convenience, let
d=1
and

\nu=2
.
For any

t
and

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}
, define

\displaystyle\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}),

\displaystyle Q_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Lambda}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\int_{0}^{t}I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds,

\displaystyle D_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\left[1-I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\right]ds.

Then

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}=\sqrt{nh}Q_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-\sqrt{nh}D_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}).

We first show that
\sqrt{nh}D_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\to 0
in
probability uniformly. It suffices to show that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left[1-I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\right]\overset{p}{\to}0,

which is equivalent to show that for any
\epsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left(\sqrt{nh}\left[1-I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\right]>\epsilon\right){\to}0.

Now for
\epsilon\geq\sqrt{nh}
, the above automatically holds.
For
\epsilon<\sqrt{nh}
, this is equivalent to show

\displaystyle\Pr\left\{n^{-1}\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\leq 0\right\}{\to}0.

(32)

Because

n^{-1}\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})=f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}+O_{p}\{h^{2}+(nh)^{-1/2}\}
,
(
32
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\Pr\left[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}+O_{p}\{h^{2}+(nh)^{-1/2}\}\leq 0\right]{\to}0,

which automatically holds under Condition
C3
and

C5
.
Hence
\sqrt{nh}D_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\to 0
in
probability uniformly.
Second we inspect the asymptotic property of

\sqrt{nh}Q_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
.
Recall
M_{i}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
is the martingale corresponding
to the
counting process
N_{i}(s)
and satisfies

dM_{i}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})=dN_{i}(s)-Y_{i}(s)\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds
.

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}Q_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

(34)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{1}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dN_{i}(s)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\leq 0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dN_{i}(s)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dN_{i}(s)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\leq 0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dN_{i}(s).

In (
), it leads to the same martingale
dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}=dN_{i}(t)-Y_{i}(s)[\lambda_{T}(t-W,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})I(W\leq t)+\lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\{1-I(W\leq t)\}]ds
in either group of transplant or nontransplant because one of
\lambda_{T}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
and
\lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
is zero.

We decompose (
) as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}

(35)

\displaystyle\quad\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{1/n\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}

\displaystyle\quad K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}\left[\frac{1}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}\right.

\displaystyle-\frac{1/n\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}+\frac{1}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\left.+O_{p}\{h^{4}+(nh)^{-1}\}\right]\times K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle Q_{n1}-Q_{n2}+o_{p}(1),

where

\displaystyle Q_{n1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\},

\displaystyle Q_{n2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]

\displaystyle\times K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n^{3}}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]

\displaystyle\times K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\},

and the remaining term in (
35
) is
o_{p}(1)

because

\sqrt{n/h}O_{p}\{h^{4}+(nh)^{-1}\}=O_{p}\{n^{1/2}h^{7/2}+(nh^{3})^{-1/2}\}=o_{p}(1)
by Condition

C2
.

Using the U-statistic property,
Q_{n2}
has leading order terms

Q_{n21}+Q_{n22}-Q_{n23}
, where

\displaystyle Q_{n21}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}E\Bigg(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\times dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}\mid\Delta_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\Bigg),

\displaystyle Q_{n22}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}E\Bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\times dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}\mid\Delta_{j},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j},Z_{j},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\Bigg),

\displaystyle Q_{n23}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}E\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]

\displaystyle\times E\left\{K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}\right\}\Bigg).

I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}=I\left[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}+O_{p}\{h^{2}+(nh)^{-1}\}>0\right]=1

almost surely. Thus, almost surely,

\displaystyle Q_{n21}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times E\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]

\displaystyle\times K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{O(h^{2})K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle 0.

uniformly as
h\to 0
.
Similarly, almost surely,

\displaystyle Q_{n22}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{f^{2}_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E^{2}\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left[Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right]

\displaystyle\times E[K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0.

Obviously,
Q_{n23}=E(Q_{n22})=0
,
hence
Q_{n2}\to 0
in probability uniformly as
n\to\infty
.

For (
34
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\sqrt{nh}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\leq 0\}}{\sum_{j=1}^{n}Y_{j}(s)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})dN_{i}(s)\to 0

in probability uniformly.
We have obtained

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}Q_{n}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\right\}}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

\displaystyle\quad\times dM_{i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}

\displaystyle+o_{p}(1).

Applying martingale
central limit theorem on (
),
we have

\displaystyle\frac{h}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}K^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)ds

(37)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}hK^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{i}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\int K^{2}(u)du+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}h^{-1/2}+h^{2})\right]ds

\displaystyle\overset{p}{\to}

\displaystyle\int K^{2}(u)du\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}).

Next we inspect the following integration for any
\epsilon>0
.

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{h}{n}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}K^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\times I\left[\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\left|\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right|>\epsilon\right]\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(hK^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\times I\left[\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\left|\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right|>\epsilon\right]\Bigg)\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}hK^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\sup_{1\leq i\leq n}I\left[\left|f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right|<\frac{\xi_{i}}{\epsilon\sqrt{nh}}\right]ds,

where

\xi_{i}=|I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}K\{(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})/h\}|
,
which is bounded following Condition
C1
.
In the above display,

\displaystyle\sup_{1\leq i\leq n}I\left[\left|f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{j}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\right|<\frac{\xi_{i}}{\epsilon\sqrt{nh}}\right]=0

as long as
n
is large enough because the right hand side
converges to 0 by
Condition
C2
but the left hand side will be
always
larger than 0 by conditions
C3
and

C5
.
On the other hand,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}hK^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z_{i}\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}\int K^{2}(u)du+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}h^{-1/2}+h^{2})

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle 0

in probability uniformly.
Hence

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{h}{n}\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{[f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}]^{2}}K^{2}_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})Y_{i}(s)

\displaystyle\times I\left[\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\left|\frac{I\{\phi_{n}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})>0\}}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})E\{I(Z\geq s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}\}}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right|>\epsilon\right]\lambda(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})ds=0

(38)

with probability 1 uniformly for any
\epsilon>0
.

In summary

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\Lambda}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}\to N\{0,\sigma^{2}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}

uniformly.
∎

3.2
Proof of Theorem
1

Because the result regarding (
3
) is the most
difficult to
establish, we provide only the proof concerning (
3
),
the result concerning (
3
) is based on a similar proof.

For each
n
, let
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}
satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\frac{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\widehat{\lambda}\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{\widehat{E}\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{\widehat{E}\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]={\bf 0}.

Under condition

, there exists a subsequence

,

,

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}+O_{p}\{(nbh^{3})^{-1/2}+h^{2}+b^{2}\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}+O_{p}\{(nbh)^{-1/2}+h^{2}+b^{2}\}}

\displaystyle=

under Condition

. Thus,

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\frac{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\widehat{\lambda}\{Z_{i},\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

under conditions
C1
-
C2
and

C6
. Note that

\displaystyle E\left(\Delta\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z,{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq Z),WI(W\leq Z)\}}{\lambda\{Z,{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq Z),WI(W\leq Z)\}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{l}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{l}Y(Z)\mid{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq Z),WI(W\leq Z)\right\}}{E\left\{Y(Z)\mid{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}}^{\rm T}{\bf X},I(W\leq Z),WI(W\leq Z)\right\}}\right]\right)

is a nonrandom quantity that does not depend on
n
, hence it is zero.
Thus the uniqueness requirement in Condition
C7

ensures that

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}
.

We show by contradiction that the subsequence that converges includes all but a
finite number of
n
’s. If this were not true, we could
obtain an infinite sequence of
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{n}
’s that did not converge
to

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}
. As this infinite sequence was in a compact set
\cal B
, we
could
obtain another subsequence that converged, say to

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{**}\neq\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}
. Using prior derivations would lead to

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{**}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}
, a contradiction to

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{**}\neq\boldsymbol{\beta}^{*}
.
Thus we conclude

\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}\to{\bf 0}

in probability when
n\to\infty
under Conditions

C1
-
C6
.
∎

\displaystyle\left.\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)\otimes{\bf X}_{li}^{\rm T}\right\}.

Now let
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}(Z,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
be the
leave-one-out version of
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}(Z,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X})
, i.e.
it
is constructed the same as
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}(Z,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X})

except
that the
i
th observation is not used.
Obviously,

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{i}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\partial}{\partial({\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)\otimes{\bf X}_{li}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{i}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{\partial}{\partial({\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)\otimes{\bf X}_{li}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

Let
E_{i}
mean taking expectation with respect to the
i
th
observation conditional on all other observations, then

\displaystyle E_{i}\left\{\frac{\Delta_{i}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\frac{\partial}{\partial({\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)\otimes{\bf X}_{li}^{\rm T}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E_{i}\left\{\frac{\partial}{\partial\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}}\int\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq s),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq s)\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq s),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq s)\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq s),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq s)\right\}}\right]E\{Y_{i}(s)\mid{\bf X}_{i}\}ds\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial}{\partial\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}}E_{i}\left\{\int\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq s),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq s)\}\right.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0}.

The last equality holds because the integrand has expectation zero
conditional on

and all other observations, and the third to last

.
Therefore, the term in (
) converges in probability
uniformly to

\displaystyle E\left\{\frac{\Delta_{i}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{\partial}{\partial({\bf X}_{i}^{\rm T}\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0})}\left(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2,-i}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)\otimes{\bf X}_{li}^{\rm T}\right\}=0

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-n^{1/2}E\left(\Delta_{i}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\otimes E\left[-\frac{K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{lj}I(Z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.+\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})I(Z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})[E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}]^{2}}\right]\right).

Here we used U-statistic property in the last equality.
Now when

nh^{4}\to 0
,

\displaystyle{\bf T}_{31}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

\displaystyle\otimes\left[-\frac{E\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle+\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{[E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}]^{2}}

\displaystyle\left.\times E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}\right]+O(n^{1/2}h^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

Thus,
{\bf T}_{33}=o_{p}(1)
as well.
Also,

\displaystyle{\bf T}_{32}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left(\Delta_{i}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\otimes\left[-\frac{K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}){\bf X}_{lj}I(Z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})E\left\{I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.\left.+\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{l}I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})I(Z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})[E\left\{I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}]^{2}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\mid\Delta_{j},Z_{j},{\bf X}_{j},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left\{E\left(\Delta_{i}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\otimes\left[-\frac{{\bf x}_{lj}I(z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})E\left\{I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{l}I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}I(z_{j}\geq Z_{i})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})[E\left\{I(Z\geq Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},Z_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}]^{2}}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right)K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j}-\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}E\left(\int_{0}^{z_{j}}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\}}{E\left\{S_{c}(s,{\bf X})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j}\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\otimes\left[\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{l}S_{c}(s,{\bf X})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j}\right\}}{E\left\{S_{c}(s,{\bf X})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j}\right\}}-{\bf x}_{lj}\right]S_{c}(s,{\bf X}_{i})ds\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}=\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j}\right)+O_{p}(n^{1/2}h^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\int Y_{j}(s)\lambda\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\}\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\}}{\lambda\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\}}

\displaystyle\otimes\left[\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{lj}Y_{j}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{j}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x}_{j},I(w_{i}\leq s),w_{i}I(w_{i}\leq s)\right\}}-{\bf x}_{lj}\right]ds+O_{p}(n^{1/2}h^{2}).

When
nh^{4}\to 0
, plugging the results of
{\bf T}_{1}
and
{\bf T}_{32}
to
(
45
), the expression in (
)
is

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\frac{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\widehat{\lambda}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{\widehat{E}\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{\widehat{E}\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq t),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq t)\}}{\lambda\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq t),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq t)\}}

\displaystyle\otimes\left[{\bf X}_{li}-\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(t)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(t)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle\times dM_{i}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq t),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq t)\}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf S}_{\rm eff}\{\Delta_{i},Z_{i},{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq t),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}+o_{p}(1).

Finally,

\displaystyle{\bf T}_{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\left[\frac{\widehat{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\widehat{\lambda}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\frac{{\boldsymbol{\lambda}}_{2}\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}{\lambda\{Z_{i},\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\}}\right]

\displaystyle\times\left[\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\widehat{E}\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{\widehat{E}\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\left(n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i}\left[\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\frac{\widehat{E}\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{\widehat{E}\left\{Y_{i}(Z_{i})\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\left(n^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int Y_{i}(s)\lambda\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq s),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq s)\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\left[\frac{E\left\{{\bf X}_{li}Y_{i}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}{E\left\{Y_{i}(s)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i},I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i}),W_{i}I(W_{i}\leq Z_{i})\right\}}-{\bf x}_{li}\right]ds\right)+o_{p}(n^{1/2}h^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1),

where the last equality holds because the integrand has mean zero
conditional on
\boldsymbol{\beta}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf X}
, and the second to last equality follows
with the same derivation of
{\bf T}_{3}
.
Using these results in (
), combined with the
results on
(
42
), the theorem holds.
∎

3.4
Proof of Theorem
3

We first analyze
\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})
. We only need to analyze

\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}

because
\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-{\widehat{m}}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}=O_{p}(\sqrt{h})=o_{p}(1)
based on Theorems

1
and
2
.

We expand

\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}

as

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}

(46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{e^{{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

\displaystyle+\sqrt{nh}e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{e^{-{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}ds.

(47)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{nh}\left\{e^{{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{e^{-{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}ds.

(48)

It is easy to see that the term in (
48
) satisfies

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{e^{{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{e^{-{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}O_{p}\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}O_{p}\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(\sqrt{nh})O_{p}\{h^{4}+(nh)^{-1}\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1),

by Condition
C2
.

We inspect the terms in (
46
) and
(
47
).
For (
46
), based on Lemma

1
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left\{e^{{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\left(\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})+O_{p}[\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}^{2}]\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+o_{p}(1),

where the last step uses Condition

C2
.

For (
47
), using Condition
C2

as well, we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{e^{-{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}-e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\right\}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left[\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})+O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1}+h^{4}\}\right]e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+o_{p}(1).

Now combine the leading terms in (
46
) and
(
47
) and use the expansion of

\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})
in Lemma

2
,

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}e^{\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-{\Lambda_{N}}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

(49)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{nh}e^{{\Lambda_{N}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{t}^{\tau}\left\{\widehat{\Lambda}_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{0}^{\tau}\sqrt{\frac{h}{n}}\frac{I\left\{\phi_{n}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})>0\right\}K_{h}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{I(r<t)\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+\int_{\max(r,t)}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds\right\}dM_{i}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})+o_{p}(1).

Note that
I\left\{\phi_{n}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})>0\right\}=1
almost
surely and
according to Lemma
1

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}hK_{h}^{2}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}_{i}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})Y_{i}(r)=f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}\}\int K^{2}(u)du+o_{p}(1).

The leading term in
(
49
) converges to

N\{0,\sigma^{2}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}
uniformly by martingale
central limit
theorem,
where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{2\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{\lambda_{N}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}\}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{I(r<t)\int_{t}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+\int_{\max(r,t)}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds\right\}^{2}dr.

Therefore

\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})-{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\right\}\to N\{0,\sigma^{2}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}
uniformly for all
t
and

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}
.

Similarly,
\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)-{m}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)\right\}\to N\{0,\sigma^{2}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)\}
uniformly for all
t
and

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}
where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{2\Lambda_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},W}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{\lambda_{T}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w\}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{I(r<t-w)\int_{t-w}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{\max(r,t-w)}^{\tau}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds\right\}^{2}dr.

Furthermore,
\widehat{m}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq t),wI(w\leq t)\}=\widehat{m}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})I(w\leq t)+\widehat{m}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\{1-I(w\leq t)\}
and
\sqrt{nh}\left\{\widehat{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq t),wI(w\leq t)\}-{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq t),wI(w\leq t)\}\right\}\to N[0,\sigma^{2}_{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq t),wI(w\leq t)\}]
uniformly for all
t
and

\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}
where

\sigma^{2}_{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq t),wI(w\leq t)\}=\sigma^{2}_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})I(w\leq t)+\sigma^{2}_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\{1-I(w\leq t)\}
.

∎

4
Relaxation of the Complete Follow-up Assumption

To weaken the complete follow-up assumption that the event time is supported on
[0,\tau]
, we relax the compact
support assumption on the event time, while allow a sample size dependent end of
the study time
\tau_{n}
. Because the estimation and inference of

\boldsymbol{\beta},\Lambda\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}
does not rely on the complete follow-up
assumption, hence under the weakened assumptions,
the same analysis as in
the main text leads to the same results for
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}

as in Theorems
1
and
2
.

We assume

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{n}}^{\infty}e^{-\Lambda\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq s),wI(w\leq s)\}}ds=o(n^{\frac{1-2\nu}{4\nu}})e^{-\Lambda\{\tau_{n}-\tau_{0},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0}),wI(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0})\}},

(50)

where
\tau_{0}
is a small positive constant.
(
50
) is equivalent to

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\tau_{n}^{\prime}e^{\Lambda\{\tau_{n}-\tau_{0},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0}),wI(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0})\}-\Lambda\{\tau_{n},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq\tau_{n}),wI(w\leq\tau_{n})\}}}{\frac{2\nu-1}{4\nu}n^{\frac{1-6\nu}{4\nu}}+n^{\frac{1-2\nu}{4\nu}}\lambda\{\tau_{n}-\tau_{0},\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0}),wI(w\leq\tau_{n}-\tau_{0})\}\tau_{n}^{\prime}}=0,

where
\tau_{n}^{\prime}\equiv d\tau_{n}/dn
.
Clearly, a sufficient condition for (
50
) to hold is that the
cumulative hazard function
\Lambda(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\geq C(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})t^{1+\alpha}
for some constant
\alpha>0
, and

\tau_{n}=n^{\omega}
for some constant
\omega>0
. We point out two facts
about this condition. First,
\omega
can be very small as long as it
is fixed, for example, we can set
\omega=1/2
. This implies that
the end of the study time
\tau_{n}
, although required to increase with sample
size
n
, can increase very slowly. Second, the tail
condition on the distribution family of
T
given
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}
is very weak.
For example, all sub-Gaussian distributions satisfy this
requirement, this naturally includes all the Cox proportional hazard
model with the baseline hazard
t^{c}
for
c>0
and the Weibull family
with increasing risk, which are often used as baseline to generate
various survival models in practice.

Let

\displaystyle m^{*}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle e^{\Lambda\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}}\int_{t}^{\tau_{n}}e^{-\Lambda\{s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq s),WI(W\leq s)\}}ds.

Then, under Condition
C2
,

\displaystyle|m\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}-m^{*}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}|

(51)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(n^{\frac{1-2\nu}{4\nu}})=o\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}.

Note that our estimator described in the main text, when viewed as an estimator
for
m^{*}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}
,
satisfies the same properties as described
in Theorem
3
, i.e.

\sqrt{nh}[\widehat{m}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}-m^{*}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}]/\sigma_{m^{*}}\{t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}\to N(0,1)

in distribution when
n\to\infty
, where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{m^{*}}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(w\leq t)e^{2\Lambda_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},W}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\int_{0}^{\tau_{n}}\frac{\lambda_{T}(r-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{I(r<t-w)\int_{t-w}^{\tau_{n}}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds+\int_{\max(r,t-w)}^{\tau_{n}}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds\right\}dr

\displaystyle+\{1-I(w\leq t)\}e^{2\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{0}^{\tau_{n}}\frac{\lambda_{N}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{I(r<t)\int_{t}^{\tau_{n}}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+\int_{\max(r,t)}^{\tau_{n}}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds\right\}dr.

In fact, the proof of Theorem
3
goes through
by substituting
\tau
with
\tau_{n}
.
In combination with (
51
) and the fact that
\sigma_{m^{*}}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})
converges to

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle I(w\leq t)e^{2\Lambda_{T}(t-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X},W}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda_{T}(r-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{I(r<t-w)\int_{t-w}^{\infty}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds+\int_{\max(r,t-w)}^{\infty}e^{-\Lambda_{T}(s-w,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x},w)}ds\right\}dr

\displaystyle+\{1-I(w\leq t)\}e^{2\Lambda_{N}(t,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\frac{\int K^{2}(u)du}{f_{\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf X}}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}\int_{0}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda_{N}(r,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}{E\{I(Z\geq r)\mid\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x}\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{I(r<t)\int_{t}^{\infty}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds+\int_{\max(r,t)}^{\infty}e^{-\Lambda_{N}(s,\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\rm T}{\bf x})}ds\right\}dr.

This further leads to

\sqrt{nh}[\widehat{m}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}-m\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}]\to N[0,\sigma_{m}^{2}\{t,\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf x},I(W\leq t),WI(W\leq t)\}]
in distribution when
n\to\infty
.
